Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got another *Zendikar*-*Zendikar*-*Worldwake* draft from a Premier Event Top 8. Enjoy!

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/act403_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20100317000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=1019256) 

Wanna see the decks they made? Click here!
